# Chapter 8-g — Group F: C-LEARN

**Document:** `NH_MASTER-21_SYSTEM_BEHAVIOR_v0_1_CANDIDATE__CH08-g.md`  
**Status:** CANDIDATE  
**Source repository:** `nesgeva/NH-GOVERNANCE`  
**Source commit:** `6a7160ba688ba4e433a31899162815df7e2bab17`  

This piece owns the cross-cutting personal-learning arc: continuous whole-mechanism access, moment-specific response calculation, thirteen shared-material views with accepted names, staged automatic/approved change, evidence-based calibration transition, protected core, later reread and learning-wide prohibitions. BOP, LMAC, OOP, Meaning Engine, authority, privacy, firmness and operation mechanics retain their existing canonical owners and IDs; no parallel store, response rulebook, new purpose vocabulary or threshold is created. Security/voice and wellbeing/interface detail remain CH09/10; full path assembly CH11 and final registers CH12. This completes the Writing 2 component sequence.

Citation keys: V10 = `01_AUTHORITATIVE/NH_MASTER-20_CORRECTED_v10.md`; MAP = `02_WORKING_MAP/NH_COMPLETE_DESIGN_AND_WIRING_MAP_v1_6_CANDIDATE.md`; DD = `01_AUTHORITATIVE/NH_DECISION_DEFAULTS-S19_v2_2.md`; `04/` = `04_ACCEPTED_STANDALONE_DESIGNS/`; `05/` = `05_ACTIVE_CANDIDATE/`; NHD identifiers locate decisions in the pinned index and do not replace behavior citations.

| C-LEARN — Personal Learning and Adaptation (§26, cross-cutting) | C-LMAC.6 — Response-time live-context query | The current six-part response context. | Recalculates response behavior for this moment under the full adaptation rules. | No response configuration or disconnected Ness model is stored. | DESIGNED | [V10 §26.7] |

## Scope, paths and source dispositions

CH08-g places the learning-owned behavior of V10 §§26.1–26.12. The full BOP, LMAC and OOP source sections were read, but their detailed records, event vocabularies, query fields, capture identities, write fences, retry/recovery and imported-reaction atoms keep the established CH08-c/d/e IDs. The learning loop consumes those owners without generating a second schema, writer, query interface, feedback store or kernel. All three prior root consumption commitments are fulfilled, and both prior incoming continuation places have individual reciprocal rows.

Every function remains connected before, during and after execution. The nine live interfaces are reused through their canonical LMAC query cards. Relevance may control attention, retrieval order, depth, immediate weighting and computational priority but cannot create an architectural boundary. Changed branches, roots, clashes and stale state permit current requery. BOP/OOP are raw-input responsibilities, not live-query targets; LMAC queries shared roots/readings. The cycle is continuous and cumulative, never restarted or replaced by a summary.

Response calculation consumes all six current context classes through C-LMAC.6.1–6.6. Pattern readings remain evidence weighted by confidence and firmness. Differently directed patterns are navigated using the whole live picture. No RBCS or equivalent stored response rulebook exists. The source requirement for Meaning Engine/Computed View/authority handling of behavioral change is preserved alongside the explicit prohibition on unprompted disclosure of the internal Computed View. Neither statement imposes individual approval on every small automatic adjustment.

The thirteen accepted area names each have a card, including the full Processing depth preferences parenthetical. Accepted Bundle 6 policy A11 and its receipt close the older V10/map working-name placeholder. These remain views into shared material, not thirteen modules, stores, models or fixed profiles. The names do not become thirteen new purpose_type values: existing controlled purpose and unknown-purpose rules remain authoritative, and the exact mapping from personalization domains to that vocabulary is an explicit open mechanical slot.

Automatic-change stages remain distinct. Every proposal exists as a shared reading before change. Small low-stakes reversible tone/timing/pacing/answer-length adaptations may occur automatically, including during learning, without approval for each adjustment. Major learning-period changes require requested approval. After evidence-based transition, a larger automatic change requires all five conditions: readings from multiple independent sessions, varied conditions, consistent direction, no contradiction from recent evidence and confidence passing the current automatic-action threshold. All four accountability properties apply to every automatic change; the producing reading and specific roots remain traceable, prior state is preserved and Ness may request reversion. The canonical protected-core atom retains safety, ownership, authority and architectural integrity outside automatic rewriting regardless of evidence strength.

Calibration uses conservative thresholds, explicit provisional confidence and lower early confidence. The month-two/month-twelve comparison is an illustration, not a threshold schedule. Psychiatric appointment records calibrate wellbeing only; identity proof, voice training and access control remain excluded. Leaving calibration requires sufficient reliability across all thirteen areas: enough independent sessions, varied conditions, consistent patterns and rereads surviving without contradiction. This four-part transition test is distinct from the five-part later-change test. There is no fixed end date: 6–10 months is an estimate, with earlier/later transition dependent on the evidence and frequency/variety of use. Later evidence from hours, days, weeks or longer can reread and overturn patterns after transition without altering the original roots/readings.

Architecture, data, behavior and evidence prohibitions are all explicit. Shared operational records remain protected, connected, append-only and one per real operation, never recursive or an additional evidence vote. Complete identity/security/calibration-profile detail remains CH09, wellbeing and interfaces CH10; no identity-training rule is imported into personal learning merely because both sources discuss calibration. Full side paths belong CH11 and final registers CH12.

Discovery covered part ID, name and topic in accepted/active/inactive sources, excluding the recovery ledger. Bundle 6 policy, mechanics, closeout and receipts cover the actual learning seam. Defaults and the decision records supplied no additional learning-specific behavior. Companion’s learning-period hit is SIA profile calibration owned by later chapters; B16’s hit concerns identifier namespaces, and the kernel hit is a source-list/governing-law reference. Those scoped discoveries confer no new learning behavior or whole-file credit. The policy receipt was read whole and already carried as read; the 58-file pending whole-read inventory is unchanged. Older A10/A11/A12 open-item wording is not reopened: accepted A10 uses C-BOP.1.1, A11 is placed here, and accepted A12’s complete reaction mechanics stay at C-BOP.14 with the C-AFFIRM.9 boundary.

## Plain-gate and empty-box review

All sixty-three cards were compared with the source-first outline. The new area-name and reliability-predicate atoms are nouns/conditions, not disconnected execution steps. Operational cards name their existing Catalog, engine, query, outcome, authority, privacy, reread or record owner. No approval gate is invented for every small adaptation; the permanent protected-core and learning-period major-change requirements remain explicit. All USED BY rows identify one place. Numeric thresholds, new purpose values and runtime mechanisms are not fabricated. Designed source behavior and accepted area-name/session-policy atoms keep their own statuses; no BUILT claim is added.

| Card | Plain gate justification |
|---|---|
